# Workspace MCP tool dictionary

Generated by `pnpm exec tsx scripts/workspace-agent-dictionary.ts`. Checked against live MCP discovery in the test suite. Descriptions and JSON input schemas are the executable interface contract; this file is discovery evidence, not proof that a query or provider operation succeeds. Clock-derived defaults are labeled <runtime date default>.

Read [capabilities and verification](workspace-agent-capabilities.md) for policy, examples, owner-only exclusions, and actual runtime evidence. Existing compatibility tools are listed explicitly and hidden by default.

## acknowledge_finance_conversation_batch

Acknowledge only the exact delivered message IDs from this existing Finance run's fixed batch. The server validates original host authority and delivery receipts. This does not move the human rendered/read cursor, approve a financial change or consume later messages.

- Domain: finances; stage: verify; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): finances:maintain, finances:write, finances:execute.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":true,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/finances.ts); typed API methods: `acknowledgeFinanceConversationBatch`.

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "runId": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid",
      "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$",
      "description": "nohmi object identifier"
    },
    "messageIds": {
      "maxItems": 100,
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "format": "uuid",
        "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$"
      }
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "runId",
    "messageIds"
  ],
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

## activate_mail_rule

Activate the exact saved rule version and current bounded preview. Preserve the returned sample fingerprint and preview timestamp; stale evidence is rejected. Requires mail:write. Agents also require mail:execute and enabled review bypass.

- Domain: mail; stage: commit; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): mail:write, mail:execute.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":false,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/mail-management.ts); typed API methods: `activateMailRule`.

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "expectedCandidateIds": {
      "maxItems": 200,
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "format": "uuid",
        "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$"
      }
    },
    "expectedPreviewFingerprint": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[a-f0-9]{64}$"
    },
    "expectedPreviewedAt": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "string",
          "format": "date-time",
          "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))T(?:(?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d:[0-5]\\d(?:\\.\\d+)?(?:Z|([+-](?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d)))$"
        },
        {
          "type": "string",
          "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))T(?:(?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d(?:Z|([+-](?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d)))$"
        }
      ]
    },
    "expectedVersion": {
      "type": "integer",
      "exclusiveMinimum": 0,
      "maximum": 9007199254740991
    },
    "ruleId": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid",
      "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$"
    }
  },
  "required": [

    "expectedPreviewFingerprint",
    "expectedPreviewedAt",
    "expectedVersion",
    "ruleId"

  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema"
}
```

## add_finance_transaction

Add a manual ledger transaction. Leave category null only when genuinely uncertain so maintenance can reason about it.

- Domain: finances; stage: commit; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): finances:write.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":false,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/finances.ts); typed API methods: `createFinanceTransaction`.

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "accountId": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid",
      "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$",
      "description": "nohmi object identifier"
    },
    "amount": {
      "type": "number",
      "exclusiveMinimum": 0
    },
    "category": {
      "default": null,
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "string",
          "maxLength": 80
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ]
    },
    "date": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "date",
      "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))$"
    },
    "direction": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "income",
        "expense",
        "transfer"
      ]
    },
    "merchant": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 240
    },
    "notes": {
      "default": null,
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "string",
          "maxLength": 4000
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ]
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "accountId",
    "amount",
    "date",
    "direction",
    "merchant"
  ],
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema"
}
```

## answer_finance_question

Answer a bounded Finance question with evidence from the person. This never changes Finance review bypass and never approves or dismisses a queued action.

- Domain: finances; stage: commit; policy: approve_each.
- Grants (all): finances:write.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":true,"idempotentHint":false,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/finances-stewardship.ts) (delegates to the named workflow helper).

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "answer": {

                "kind",

                    {
                      "type": "null"

                },
                "rationale": {
                  "type": "string",
                  "minLength": 1,
                  "maxLength": 1000
                }

                      },
```

## answer_finance_review

Record the user's answer against its exact case. A classify or link resolution applies the change and resolves the row atomically. A clarify resolution saves a durable note for the next maintenance pass and keeps the case open. Inspect context for the transaction date, account, direction, and posting status before asking a question; provide supporting context rather than an unexplained amount.

- Domain: finances; stage: commit; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): finances:write.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":true,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/finances.ts); typed API methods: `answerFinanceReview`.

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "reviewId": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid",
      "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$",
      "description": "nohmi object identifier"
    },
    "answer": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 10000
    },
    "idempotencyKey": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 200
    },
    "resolution": {
      "oneOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "categoryId": {
              "type": "string",
              "format": "uuid",
              "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$"
            },
            "type": {
              "type": "string",
              "const": "confirm_classification"
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "categoryId",
            "type"
          ]
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "categoryId": {
              "type": "string",
              "format": "uuid",
              "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$"
            },
            "meaning": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 500
            },
            "type": {
              "type": "string",
              "const": "classify_transaction"
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "categoryId",
            "meaning",
            "type"
          ]
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "relatedTransactionId": {
              "type": "string",
              "format": "uuid",
              "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$"
            },
            "relationship": {
              "type": "string",
              "enum": [
                "transfer",
                "reimbursement",
                "refund",
                "reversal",
                "duplicate"
              ]
            },
            "type": {
              "type": "string",
              "const": "link_transactions"
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "relatedTransactionId",
            "relationship",
            "type"
          ]
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "changes": {
              "type": "object",
              "propertyNames": {
                "type": "string"
              },
              "additionalProperties": {}
            },
            "type": {
              "type": "string",
              "const": "update_profile"
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "changes",
            "type"
          ]
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "rationale": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 1000
            },
            "type": {
              "type": "string",
              "const": "dismiss"
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "rationale",
            "type"
          ]
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {

              "minLength": 1,

            },
```

## answer_mail_question

Answer a stewardship question from established evidence; uncertainty is not permission to invent an answer. Requires mail:write. Agents also require mail:execute and enabled review bypass.

- Domain: mail; stage: commit; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): mail:write, mail:execute.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":false,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/mail-management.ts); typed API methods: `answerMailQuestion`.

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "answer": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 2000
    },
    "expectedVersion": {
      "type": "integer",
      "exclusiveMinimum": 0,
      "maximum": 9007199254740991
    },
    "generalize": {
      "default": false,
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "questionId": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid",
      "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "answer",
    "expectedVersion",
    "questionId"
  ],
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema"
}
```

## apply_finance_categorizations

Apply revision-guarded categorization decisions. nohmi requires evidence first, then either applies, queues one review, or asks a bounded question.

- Domain: finances; stage: commit; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): finances:write.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":false,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/finances-stewardship.ts); typed API methods: `applyFinanceCategorizations`.

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "decisions": {
      "minItems": 1,
      "maxItems": 100,
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
          "categoryId": {
            "type": "string",
            "format": "uuid",
            "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$"
          },
          "confidence": {
            "type": "number",
            "minimum": 0,
            "maximum": 1
          },
          "expectedTransactionUpdatedAt": {
            "anyOf": [
              {
                "type": "string",
                "format": "date-time",
                "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))T(?:(?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d:[0-5]\\d(?:\\.\\d+)?(?:Z|([+-](?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d)))$"
              },
```

## complete_finance_account_connection

Check provider-confirmed consent for a hosted bank connection and persist connected accounts. Returns pending until the owner finishes consent at the URL from start_finance_account_connection. Never accepts or returns provider credentials. Safe to poll; uncertain token exchanges require fresh authorization. Requires finances:write, finances:execute and enabled review bypass for agents.

- Domain: finances; stage: commit; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): finances:write, finances:execute.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":true,"openWorldHint":true}`.

```json
}
```

## complete_finance_onboarding

Complete the exact reviewed Finance setup revision after the server revalidates source, context, display review and automation choice. Later needs no host; now requires an actual authorized registration and cadence, while Chat proof remains separate. Only returned completedAt confirms completion. Requires current owner-authorized execution and never approves or activates a budget.

- Domain: finances; stage: commit; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): finances:write, finances:execute.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":true,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/finances.ts); typed API methods: `completeFinanceOnboarding`.

```json
  "properties": {
    "expectedRevision": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 9007199254740990
    },
    "idempotencyKey": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 200
    },
    "automationChoice": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "later",
        "now"
      ]
    }
  },

    "expectedRevision",
    "idempotencyKey",
    "automationChoice"
  ],

  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

## complete_reminder

Mark one loaded reminder complete or reopen it. Pass expectedUpdatedAt from get_reminder so a concurrent change fails safely. This records an audit event even when the requested state matches the current state, so it is not idempotent. This is a direct, audited mutation. nohmi's API scopes and scoped-agent policy decision are authoritative; profile preferences and MCP annotations do not grant access.

- Domain: reminders; stage: commit; policy: approve_each.
- Grants (all): reminders:write.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":false,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/reminders.ts); typed API methods: `completeReminder`.

```json
          "format": "date-time",

        },
        {
          "type": "string",
          "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))T(?:(?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d(?:Z|([+-](?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d)))$"
```

## create_automation_host_schedule

Register metadata for an externally managed schedule using this caller's existing connection and a subset of its scopes. Does not schedule the external host. Requires finances:read and finances:maintain. Agent mutations also require finances:write, finances:execute and enabled review bypass.

- Grants (all): finances:read, finances:maintain, finances:write, finances:execute.

```json
              "type": "object",

                "type": {
                  "type": "string",
                  "const": "interval"
                },
                "everyMinutes": {
                  "type": "integer",
                  "minimum": 1,
                  "maximum": 10080
                },
                "timeZone": {
                  "type": "string",
                  "minLength": 1,
                  "maxLength": 100
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "type",
                "everyMinutes",
                "timeZone"
              ],
              "additionalProperties": false
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "type",
            "recurrence"
          ],
          "additionalProperties": false
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "tenantAuthorizationConnectionId",
        "label",
        "requestedScopes",
        "hostSurface",
        "trigger"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": false
    },
    {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "tenantAuthorizationConnectionId": {
          "type": "string",
          "format": "uuid",
          "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$"
        },
        "label": {
          "type": "string",
          "minLength": 1,
          "maxLength": 100
        },
        "requestedScopes": {
          "minItems": 1,
          "maxItems": 28,
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "knowledge:read",
              "knowledge:write",
              "reminders:read",
              "reminders:write",
              "tasks:read",
              "tasks:write",
              "tasks:execute",
              "calendar:read",
              "calendar:write",
              "calendar:execute",
              "mail:read",
              "mail:write",
              "mail:execute",
              "goals:read",
              "goals:write",
              "tracking:read",
              "tracking:write",
              "automations:read",
              "automations:write",
              "audit:read",
              "finances:read",
              "finances:write",
              "finances:execute",
              "finances:maintain",
              "bookmarks:read",
              "texting:read",
              "texting:write",
              "texting:execute"
            ]
          }
        },
        "hostSurface": {
          "type": "string",
          "const": "claude_code_routine"
        },
        "trigger": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "type": "object",
              "properties": {
                "type": {
                  "type": "string",
                  "const": "event"
                },
                "expectedMaximumLatencyMinutes": {
                  "type": "integer",
                  "minimum": 1,
                  "maximum": 60
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "type",
                "expectedMaximumLatencyMinutes"
              ],
              "additionalProperties": false
            },
            {
              "type": "object",
              "properties": {
                "type": {
                  "type": "string",
                  "const": "scheduled"
                },
                "localTimes": {
                  "minItems": 1,
                  "maxItems": 8,
                  "type": "array",
                  "items": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "pattern": "^(?:[01][0-9]|2[0-3]):[0-5][0-9]$"
                  }
                },
                "timeZone": {
                  "type": "string",
                  "minLength": 1,
                  "maxLength": 100
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "type",
                "localTimes",
                "timeZone"
              ],
              "additionalProperties": false
            }
          ]
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "tenantAuthorizationConnectionId",
        "label",
        "requestedScopes",
        "hostSurface",
        "trigger"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": false
    }
  ]
}
```

## create_calendar

Create a local calendar with a name, optional color, and IANA timezone. Returns its canonical ID for event creation. Requires calendar:write; this direct scoped operation does not require review bypass.

- Domain: calendar; stage: commit; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): calendar:write.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":false,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/calendar-management.ts); typed API methods: `createCalendar`.

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "name": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 120
    },
    "color": {
      "default": null,
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "string",
          "pattern": "^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$"
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ]
    },
    "timezone": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 100
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "name",
    "timezone"
  ],
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema"
}
```

## create_calendar_attention_item

Create or refresh one open important, upcoming, or follow-up item for an owned Calendar event. nohmi locks and validates the event, derives provider provenance and current revision, deduplicates the open event/kind pair, and never copies event notes.

- Domain: calendar; stage: commit; policy: approve_each.
- Grants (all): calendar:write.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":false,"openWorldHint":false}`.

## create_event

Create an exact event on a writable destination under the owner's granted authority. This can write to an external provider. Inspect ticket, booking, registration or accepted-commitment evidence with preview_calendar_commitment before deciding on creation; caller-supplied evidence does not itself grant authority.

- Domain: calendar; stage: commit; policy: approve_each.
- Grants (all): calendar:write.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":false,"openWorldHint":true}`.

```json
              "accepted",

          },

          }

      "format": "uuid",
```

## create_finance_account

Create a manually maintained financial account. Balance is a decimal currency amount, not integer cents. Bank authentication uses start_finance_account_connection. Requires finances:write, finances:execute and enabled review bypass for agents.

- Domain: finances; stage: commit; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): finances:write, finances:execute.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":false,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/finances-management.ts); typed API methods: `createFinanceAccount`.

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "currencyCode": {
      "default": null,
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "string",
          "pattern": "^[A-Z]{3}$"
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ]
    },
    "balance": {
      "default": null,
      "type": [
        "number",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "institution": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 160
    },
    "kind": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "cash",
        "investment",
        "debt",
        "other"
      ]
    },
    "name": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 160
    },
    "provider": {
      "default": "manual",
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "plaid",
        "paypal",
        "venmo",
        "zelle",
        "manual"
      ]
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "institution",
    "name"
  ],
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema"
}
```

## create_finance_budget

Create and show a complete balanced proposal. Use explicit income, reserve draw, or borrowing; never hide a deficit or leave resources unassigned. This does not activate it.

- Domain: finances; stage: commit; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): finances:write.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":true,"openWorldHint":false}`.

## create_finance_budget_policy

Save a draft budget policy with an idempotency key. This does not enable automatic execution. Requires finances:write. Agents also require finances:execute and enabled review bypass.

- Domain: finances; stage: commit; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): finances:write, finances:execute.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":false,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/finances-policy-management.ts); typed API methods: `createFinanceBudgetPolicy`.

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "idempotencyKey": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 200
    },
    "planId": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid",
```

## create_finance_category

Create a named financial category; inspect get_finance_categories first to avoid duplicates. Category icons are assigned automatically from meaning; do not ask about or narrate cosmetic icon choices unless the user requests them. Requires finances:write, finances:execute and enabled review bypass for agents.

- Domain: finances; stage: commit; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): finances:write, finances:execute.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":false,"openWorldHint":false}`.

```json
    }
```

## create_reminder

Create one reminder immediately. dueAt is a due or attention time; it does not schedule a separate notification. Include an IANA timezone when local-time meaning matters. This is a direct, audited mutation. nohmi's API scopes and scoped-agent policy decision are authoritative; profile preferences and MCP annotations do not grant access.

- Domain: reminders; stage: commit; policy: approve_each.
- Grants (all): reminders:write.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":false,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/reminders.ts); typed API methods: `createReminder`.

```json
              "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))T(?:(?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d:[0-5]\\d(?:\\.\\d+)?(?:Z|([+-](?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d)))$"
            },
            {
              "type": "string",
              "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))T(?:(?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d(?:Z|([+-](?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d)))$"
            }
          ],
          "description": "ISO 8601 date-time with offset"
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ]
    },
    "notes": {
      "default": null,
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "string",
          "maxLength": 10000
        },
        {
          "type": "null"

      "default": "medium",

      "maxLength": 240
    }

  ],
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema"
```

## delete_event

Move an event and all linked blocks to recoverable trash locally and at connected providers. Requires the source and exact independent block revisions from get_event, and returns the deleted revisions required by restore_event. Provider deletion can cancel an attendee-facing event.

## delete_reminder

Move one loaded reminder to recoverable trash; this is not permanent deletion. Pass expectedUpdatedAt so a concurrent change fails safely. The result contains the deleted revision needed for guarded restore; audit history retains source, before/after state, actor, and policy. This is a direct, audited mutation. nohmi's API scopes and scoped-agent policy decision are authoritative; profile preferences and MCP annotations do not grant access.

- Domain: reminders; stage: commit; policy: approve_each.
- Grants (all): reminders:write.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":true,"idempotentHint":false,"openWorldHint":false}`.

## exchange_finance_conversation_challenge

Complete a human-issued Finance message-protocol challenge with the original authorized host connection: fetch the actual challenge, acknowledge its exact nonce and ID, then persist a harmless response. Verification is server-owned; registration, check-in and self-reported verified flags cannot unlock Chat. Challenge traffic stays outside conversation history and unread counts.

- Domain: finances; stage: verify; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): finances:maintain, finances:write, finances:execute.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":true,"openWorldHint":false}`.

## execute_finance_review_batch

Resume at most 10 pending decisions from the exact persisted preview fingerprint. Inspect returned outcomes and pendingCount before continuing. Requires finances:read, finances:write, and finances:execute; the API rechecks current authorization, policy, and record revisions for each decision.

- Domain: finances; stage: commit; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): finances:read, finances:write, finances:execute.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":false,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/finance-review-backlog.ts); typed API methods: `executeFinanceReviewBatch`.

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "fingerprint": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^sha256:[a-f0-9]{64}$"
    },
    "limit": {
      "default": 10,
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 10
    },
    "id": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid",
      "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "fingerprint",
    "id"
  ],
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

## get_finance_cashflow

Read legacy forecast income, obligations and alerts in their existing owner-wide scope. For qualified near-term advice, supply an explicit window to read the canonical occurrence/match evidence and qualified payday projection; preserve unavailable reasons and unknown amounts. Raw supporting context is separate from that qualified projection: current schedules and balance statements are account-scoped, and balance-statement sources cover capped all-known account history rather than only the calendar window. Preserve returned coverage/truncation flags; these independently read context records are not an atomic projection snapshot. Schedule/match, goal-progress and balance confirmations require the owner or an execution agent with enabled review bypass; preserve the actual confirming actor and never fabricate evidence.

- Domain: finances; stage: inspect; policy: read_only.
- Grants (all): finances:read.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":true,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":true,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/finances.ts); typed API methods: `listFinanceAlerts`, `getFinanceForecast`, `listFinanceIncomeStreams`, `listFinanceRecurringObligations`, `getFinanceCashflowProjection`, `listFinanceRecurringSchedules`, `listFinanceBalanceBases`, `listFinanceBalanceBasisSources`, `listFinanceCashflowSources`.

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "window": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "from": {
          "type": "string",
          "format": "date",
          "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))$"
        },
        "to": {
          "type": "string",
          "format": "date",
          "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))$"
        },
        "accountIds": {
          "maxItems": 100,
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "string",
            "format": "uuid",
            "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$"
          }
        },
        "selectedIncomeStreamId": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "type": "string",
              "format": "uuid",
              "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$"
            },
            {
              "type": "null"
            }
          ]
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "from",
        "to",
        "selectedIncomeStreamId"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": false
    }
  },
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

```json
  },
```

```json
}
```

## get_finance_conversation_batch

Page only the immutable message batch captured by the existing maintain_finances run, using its original verified host connection. Follow the returned signed cursor; never scan a live queue or start another Chat job. Reading persists exact delivery receipts but does not acknowledge messages or mark them read by the human. Later messages belong to a later run.

- Domain: finances; stage: inspect; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): finances:maintain, finances:write, finances:execute.

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "runId": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid",
      "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$",
      "description": "nohmi object identifier"
    },
    "cursor": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 2000
    },

      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 100

  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

## get_finance_conversation_evidence

Read the committed financial-effect receipt for one exact existing Finance run, candidate and revision, using the original verified host with current execution authority. Follow nextCursor for bounded audit/record reference pages. This read never applies or retries effects and never reads a live conversation queue. Expired/revoked original credentials require explicit recovery.

- Domain: finances; stage: inspect; policy: read_only.
- Grants (all): finances:maintain, finances:write, finances:execute.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":true,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":true,"openWorldHint":false}`.

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "runId": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid",
      "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$",
      "description": "nohmi object identifier"
    },
    "candidateId": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid",
      "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$"
    },
    "candidateRevision": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^sha256:[a-f0-9]{64}$"
    },
    "cursor": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 3000
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "runId",
    "candidateId",
    "candidateRevision"
  ],
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

## get_finance_position

Read ownership-qualified financial position for the inclusive date range and optional canonical account IDs. Requires finances:read.

- Domain: finances; stage: inspect; policy: read_only.
- Grants (all): finances:read.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":true,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":true,"openWorldHint":false}`.

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "accountIds": {
      "maxItems": 100,
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "format": "uuid",
        "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$"
      }
    },
    "from": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "date",
      "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))$"
    },
    "through": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "date",
      "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))$"
    }
  },

    "through"
  ],

  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

## get_finance_spending

Read the Finance Spending dashboard for the exact account/category selection, dates, timezone, basis and grouping. This read never approves or changes a budget.

- Domain: finances; stage: inspect; policy: read_only.
- Grants (all): finances:read.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":true,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":true,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/finances.ts); typed API methods: `getFinanceSpending`.

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "scope": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "from": {
          "type": "string",
          "format": "date",
          "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))$"
        },
        "through": {
          "type": "string",
          "format": "date",
          "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))$"
        },
        "timezone": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "accountIds": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "type": "array",
              "items": {
                "type": "string",
                "minLength": 1
              }
            },
            {
              "type": "null"
            }
          ]
        },
        "categoryIds": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "type": "array",
              "items": {
                "anyOf": [
                  {
                    "type": "string",
                    "minLength": 1
                  },
                  {
                    "type": "null"
                  }
                ]
              }
            },
            {
              "type": "null"
            }
          ]
        },
        "basis": {
          "type": "string",
          "enum": [
            "personal_cost",
            "full_outflow"
          ]
        },
        "includePending": {
          "type": "boolean"
        },
        "subtractRefunds": {
          "type": "boolean"
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "from",
        "through",
        "timezone",
        "accountIds",
        "categoryIds",
        "basis",
        "includePending",
        "subtractRefunds"
      ]
    },
    "grouping": {
      "default": "auto",
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "auto",
        "buckets",
        "categories"
      ]
    },
    "includeUncategorized": {
      "default": true,
      "type": "boolean"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "scope"
  ],
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema"
}
```

## get_finance_status

Preferred complete-workspace Finance status operation. Read readiness, freshness, outstanding work, active or recoverable maintenance, and open questions for the selected scope. With no arguments, inspect all outstanding work; questions and approvals remain pending rather than guessed.

- Domain: finances; stage: inspect; policy: read_only.
- Grants (all): finances:read.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":true,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":true,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/finances-stewardship.ts); typed API methods: `getFinanceStatus`.

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "scope": {
      "default": {
        "type": "all_outstanding"
      },
      "oneOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "type": {
              "type": "string",
              "const": "all_outstanding"
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "type"
          ]
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "type": {
              "type": "string",
              "const": "window"
            },
            "start": {
              "type": "string",
              "format": "date",
              "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))$"
            },
            "end": {
              "type": "string",
              "format": "date",
              "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))$"
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "type",
            "start",
            "end"
          ]
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "type": {
              "type": "string",
              "const": "target"
            },
            "entityType": {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 100,
              "pattern": "^[a-z0-9]+(?:_[a-z0-9]+)*$"
            },
            "id": {
              "type": "string",
              "format": "uuid",
              "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$"
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "type",
            "entityType",
            "id"
          ]
        }
      ]
    }
  },
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

```json
{
  "type": "object",

      "type": "string",
```

```json
      "description": "nohmi object identifier"
    },
    "csv": {
      "type": "string",

      "maxLength": 1000000

}
```

## issue_finance_host_prompt

Persist the current server-issued prompt for an existing schedule with its original connection and explicit Finance execution authority. First issuance checks the schedule revision; repeating the same schedule/template returns the immutable original. This does not install instructions, change external scheduling, verify Chat, or grant financial authority.

- Domain: finances; stage: prepare; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): finances:maintain, finances:write, finances:execute.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":true,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/finances.ts); typed API methods: `issueAutomationHostPrompt`.

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "scheduleId": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid",
      "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$",
      "description": "nohmi object identifier"
    },
    "templateVersion": {
      "type": "integer",
      "exclusiveMinimum": 0,
      "maximum": 9007199254740991
    },
    "expectedScheduleVersion": {
      "type": "integer",
      "exclusiveMinimum": 0,
      "maximum": 9007199254740991
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "scheduleId",
    "templateVersion",
    "expectedScheduleVersion"
  ],
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

## list_events

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "calendarIds": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "format": "uuid",
        "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$",
        "description": "nohmi object identifier"
      }
    },
    "from": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "string",
          "format": "date-time",
          "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))T(?:(?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d:[0-5]\\d(?:\\.\\d+)?(?:Z|([+-](?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d)))$"
        },
        {
          "type": "string",
          "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))T(?:(?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d(?:Z|([+-](?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d)))$"
        }
      ],
      "description": "ISO 8601 date-time with offset"
    },
    "query": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 200
    },
    "to": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "string",
          "format": "date-time",
          "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))T(?:(?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d:[0-5]\\d(?:\\.\\d+)?(?:Z|([+-](?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d)))$"
        },
        {
          "type": "string",
          "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))T(?:(?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d(?:Z|([+-](?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d)))$"
        }
      ],
      "description": "ISO 8601 date-time with offset"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "from",
    "to"
  ],
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema"
}
```

## list_finance_spending_transactions

Read transactions from an exact Spending reference revision and bounded group/date filter. Follow its returned cursor; stale references require a fresh Spending read. This POST-backed read does not mutate records.

- Domain: finances; stage: inspect; policy: read_only.
- Grants (all): finances:read.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":true,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":true,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/finances.ts); typed API methods: `listFinanceSpendingTransactions`.

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "filter": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "query": {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "scope": {
              "type": "object",
              "properties": {
                "from": {
                  "type": "string",
                  "format": "date",
                  "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))$"
                },
                "through": {
                  "type": "string",
                  "format": "date",
                  "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))$"
                },
                "timezone": {
                  "type": "string"
                },
                "accountIds": {
                  "anyOf": [
                    {
                      "type": "array",
                      "items": {
                        "type": "string",
                        "minLength": 1
                      }
                    },
                    {
                      "type": "null"
                    }
                  ]
                },
                "categoryIds": {
                  "anyOf": [
                    {
                      "type": "array",
                      "items": {
                        "anyOf": [
                          {
                            "type": "string",
                            "minLength": 1
                          },
                          {
                            "type": "null"
                          }
                        ]
                      }
                    },
                    {
                      "type": "null"
                    }
                  ]
                },
                "basis": {
                  "type": "string",
                  "enum": [
                    "personal_cost",
                    "full_outflow"
                  ]
                },
                "includePending": {
                  "type": "boolean"
                },
                "subtractRefunds": {
                  "type": "boolean"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "from",
                "through",
                "timezone",
                "accountIds",
                "categoryIds",
                "basis",
                "includePending",
                "subtractRefunds"
              ]
            },
            "grouping": {
              "default": "auto",
              "type": "string",
              "enum": [
                "auto",
                "buckets",
                "categories"
              ]
            },
            "includeUncategorized": {
              "default": true,
              "type": "boolean"
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "scope"
          ]
        },
        "referenceRevision": {
          "type": "string",
          "pattern": "^[a-f0-9]{64}$"
        },
        "groupId": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 100
            },
            {
              "type": "null"
            }
          ]
        },
        "from": {
          "type": "string",
          "format": "date",
          "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))$"
        },
        "through": {
          "type": "string",
          "format": "date",
          "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))$"
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "query",
        "referenceRevision",
        "groupId",
        "from",
        "through"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": false
    },
    "cursor": {
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 100
    },
    "limit": {
      "default": 50,
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 100
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "filter"
  ],
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

```json
        }
      ],
      "description": "ISO 8601 date-time with offset"
    },
    "dueBefore": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "string",
          "format": "date-time",
          "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))T(?:(?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d:[0-5]\\d(?:\\.\\d+)?(?:Z|([+-](?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d)))$"
        },
        {
          "type": "string",
          "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))T(?:(?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d(?:Z|([+-](?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d)))$"
        }
      ],

      "default": 50,
```

## list_task_workspace

Read a globally filtered, sorted and grouped page of nohmi Tasks and Reminders, preserving each canonical kind and revision. Mixed discovery requires tasks:read and reminders:read. Omitted status means open in All/Today/Upcoming and all terminal or unavailable-container records in History; Trash is recoverable deletion. Today uses the person's planning timezone and includes overdue due dates. Upcoming begins after today. Exact date bounds are inclusive. groupKey is a planning-local YYYY-MM-DD, a List/Project ID, or none. readOnly marks unavailable containers outside Trash; Trash permits guarded restoration including Inbox fallback. Pass nextCursor with the same filters to continue; total counts all matching records. Due times do not promise notification delivery. Existing kind-specific tools retain least-privilege access.

- Domain: tasks; stage: inspect; policy: read_only.
- Grants (all): tasks:read, reminders:read.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":true,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":true,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/task-workspace.ts); typed API methods: `listTaskWorkspace`.

## list_workspace_sources

List owned connected sources with enabled Mail/Calendar capabilities, freshness and connection health. A source can supply multiple workspaces, so this shared read requires mail:read, calendar:read and reminders:read. Credentials are never returned. Provider login remains an owner consent step.

- Domain: assistant; stage: inspect; policy: read_only.
- Grants (all): mail:read, calendar:read, reminders:read.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":true,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":true,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/workspace-sources.ts); typed API methods: `listConnectors`.

```json
  "properties": {},

}
```

## list_x_bookmarks

List synchronized posts from the user's selected X bookmark folder. Use each post URL as source attribution when proposing a calendar event; do not create an event until its date and time are definite.

- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":true,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":true,"openWorldHint":false}`.

## maintain_finances

Start or resume durable Finance maintenance with finances:maintain. This tool never queues an automation; recurring schedules belong to the external host. Follow the returned typed next action: inspect the candidate, complete its ledger challenge, obtain required action approval, then verify the result and immutable period review. Resume persisted work by run ID; questions, approval, and recovery states remain explicit.

- Domain: finances; stage: commit; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): finances:maintain.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":false,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/finances.ts); typed API methods: `maintainFinances`.

## maintain_mail

Request one durable Mail maintenance turn. The API owns scope, authority, retries, questions, approved-rule provider effects, and honest settlement. This tool has no memory or polling loop and cannot compose, draft, reply, forward, or send email.

- Domain: mail; stage: commit; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): mail:write.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":false,"openWorldHint":true}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/mail-stewardship.ts); typed API methods: `maintainMail`.

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "scope": {
      "default": {
        "type": "all_outstanding"
      },
      "oneOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",

              "const": "all_outstanding"
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "type"
          ]
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "type": {
              "type": "string",
              "const": "window"
            },
            "start": {
              "type": "string",
              "format": "date",
              "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))$"
            },
            "end": {
              "type": "string",
              "format": "date",
              "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))$"
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "type",
            "start",
            "end"
          ]
        },
        {
          "type": "object",
          "properties": {
            "type": {
              "type": "string",

            },

            }

          ]
        }

  },
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema"
```

## manage_finance_budget_bucket

Create or update a planning bucket and its category membership. Icons are assigned automatically from meaning; do not ask about or narrate cosmetic icon choices unless the user requests them. Categories remain granular ledger labels; membership is exclusive within the active taxonomy.

- Domain: finances; stage: commit; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): finances:write.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":true,"openWorldHint":false}`.

## observe_finance_host_schedule

Record the host's observed active or paused state. Scheduled Claude routines report invocationObserved:true only during an actual invocation; generic active checks do not advance outstanding projected slots. Use the evidence timestamp, not receipt time. Only the originally bound Finance maintenance connection may report observations. This does not create, repair or reschedule host work.

- Domain: finances; stage: commit; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): finances:maintain.

## prepare_finance_budget_baseline_qualification

Prepare a durable, exact monthly baseline review for the selected budget version, accounts, currency and timezone. Reuse the idempotency key only for the same selection. Return the exact server snapshot and fingerprint for confirmation by the owner or an execution agent under enabled review bypass; preparation alone never qualifies a baseline or activates a budget.

- Domain: finances; stage: prepare; policy: preview.
- Grants (all): finances:write.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":true,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/finances.ts); typed API methods: `prepareFinanceBudgetBaselineQualification`.

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "accountIds": {
      "minItems": 1,
      "maxItems": 100,
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "format": "uuid",
        "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$"
      }
    },
    "budgetVersionId": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid",
      "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$"
    },
    "currency": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[A-Z]{3}$"
    },
    "idempotencyKey": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 200
    },
    "month": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^\\d{4}-(0[1-9]|1[0-2])$"
    },
    "timezone": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 100
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "accountIds",
    "budgetVersionId",
    "currency",
    "idempotencyKey",
    "month",
    "timezone"
  ],
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

## preview_calendar_commitment

Preview one exact Calendar candidate from caller-supplied commitment evidence. The API checks destination capability, projection freshness, exact duplicates, profile alignment, and requested policy without writing an event. Caller-supplied evidence remains unverified and can never authorize approved_rule. This is the Calendar-owned intake shape for a later durable integration; do not scan Mail here.

- Domain: calendar; stage: prepare; policy: preview.
- Grants (all): calendar:read.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":true,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":true,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/calendar.ts); typed API methods: `previewCalendarCommitment`.

```json
                      "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$"
                    },
                    {
                      "type": "null"
                    }
                  ]
                },
                "provider": {
                  "type": "string",
                  "enum": [
                    "google",
                    "icloud",
                    "local",
                    "paypal",
                    "plaid",
                    "twilio",
                    "venmo",
                    "x",
                    "zelle"
                  ]
                },

                  "type": [
                    "string",
                    "null"
                  ]
                },
                "revision": {
                  "type": [
                    "string",
                    "null"
                  ]
                },
                "sourceType": {
                  "type": "string",
                  "enum": [
                    "calendar_event",
                    "finance_account",
                    "finance_income_stream",
                    "finance_recurring_obligation",
                    "finance_transaction",
                    "mail_thread",
                    "text_message",

                "provider",
                "remoteId",
                "revision",
                "sourceType"

              "type": "string",
              "minLength": 1,
              "maxLength": 1000
            }
          },
          "required": [
            "kind",
            "source",
            "summary"
          ]
        },
        "flexibility": {
          "type": "string",
          "enum": [
            "hard",
            "flexible"
          ],
          "description": "Hard commitments are never silently rearranged; flexible remains proposal-only"
        }
      },
      "required": [
        "endsAt",
        "startsAt",
        "timezone",
        "title",
        "calendarId",
        "evidence",
        "flexibility"
      ]
    },
    "expectedProfileVersion": {
      "default": null,
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "integer",
          "exclusiveMinimum": 0,
          "maximum": 9007199254740991
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ]
    },
    "profileId": {
      "default": null,
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "string",
          "format": "uuid",
          "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$"
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ]
    },
    "requestedPolicy": {
      "default": "preview",
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "read_only",
        "preview",
        "approve_each",
        "approved_rule"
      ]
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "candidate"
  ],
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema"
}
```

## preview_finance_budget_policy

Evaluate a policy against qualified position evidence. This computes a read-only preview and never activates execution. Requires finances:read; review bypass is not required.

- Domain: finances; stage: prepare; policy: preview.
- Grants (all): finances:read.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":true,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":true,"openWorldHint":false}`.

## preview_finance_review_batch

Persist up to 100 explicit evidence-backed decisions with required timestamps and authoritative decimal-string revisions from backlog provenance: expectedTransactionRevision for every decision, plus expectedReviewRevision and expectedActionRevision for review decisions. Include an idempotency key. This creates durable preview state; it does not execute decisions or learn merchant rules. Requires finances:read and finances:write.

- Domain: finances; stage: prepare; policy: preview.
- Grants (all): finances:read, finances:write.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":true,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/finance-review-backlog.ts); typed API methods: `previewFinanceReviewBatch`.

## reconcile_mail_draft

Resolve an uncertain delivery only after checking Sent Mail evidence. This records sent or not_sent and never resends. Requires mail:write. Agents also require mail:execute and enabled review bypass.

- Domain: mail; stage: commit; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): mail:write, mail:execute.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":false,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/mail-management.ts); typed API methods: `reconcileMailDraft`.

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "outcome": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "not_sent",
        "sent"
      ]
    },
    "draftId": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid",
      "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "outcome",
    "draftId"
  ],
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema"
}
```

## recover_finance_host_run

Rebind an idle recoverable run to an active polling schedule within the original connection. First inspect the external host and current run; hostChecked must reflect actual verification. Requires finances:read, finances:maintain, finances:write, finances:execute and review bypass.

- Domain: finances; stage: recover; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): finances:read, finances:maintain, finances:write, finances:execute.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":false,"openWorldHint":false}`.

## refresh_finance_maintenance_execution_authorization

Explicitly refresh an existing submitted candidate's execution binding through proven canonical OAuth succession on its original host. Requires current maintenance, write and execution grants, policy and protocol proof. Reuse operationId only for the exact command retry: its persisted pending result stays stable. After control progression, read fresh execution facts and use a NEW operationId. Active claims remain pending until their lease deadline; never steal a claim or replay financial effects. Missing historical bindings and unrelated credentials fail closed.

- Domain: finances; stage: commit; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): finances:maintain, finances:write, finances:execute.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":true,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/finances.ts); typed API methods: `refreshFinanceMaintenanceExecutionAuthorization`.

```json
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "operationId": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid",
      "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$"
    },
    "candidateId": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid",
      "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$"
    },
    "candidateRevision": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^sha256:[a-f0-9]{64}$"
    },
    "expectedCurrentActorId": {

          "type": "string",
```

## register_agent_account

Register once on your own authenticated connection, then reuse the returned shared identity across workspaces. Repeated calls return the existing identity without merging accounts or widening scopes. Optional platform is an unverified label, not proof of provider account ownership. The API owns the immutable nohmi UUID, generated display name, avatar seed, and identitySource (connection or user_confirmed). Never guess provider account IDs or send credentials. The user handles account grouping across client surfaces in shared settings.

- Domain: assistant; stage: commit; policy: approve_each.
- Grants (any): orientation only.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":true,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/agent-account.ts); typed API methods: `registerCurrentAgentAccount`.

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "platform": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "claude",
        "codex",
        "other",
        "unknown"
      ]
    }
  },
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

## reply_to_finance_conversation_batch

Persist a contextual reply to exact source messages delivered within this existing Finance run's fixed cutoff. Reuse the idempotency key only for the same reply. Read committed effect evidence before recovering a lost reply; publishing text never authorizes or repeats a financial effect and cannot approve a budget.

- Domain: finances; stage: commit; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): finances:maintain, finances:write, finances:execute.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":true,"openWorldHint":false}`.

## resolve_finance_transaction_review

Resolve an owned transaction review: approve, recategorize, explicitly confirm a transfer, or defer. Read list_finance_reviews for reviewId, then get_finance_transaction for the exact expectedTransactionUpdatedAt. Non-deferred agent decisions must match the current server proposal from propose_finance_categorizations, including its confidence and evidence threshold. Bypass does not let an agent confirm an unresolved ambiguous transfer; defer it for owner evidence. learnMerchant always creates a reusable rule and requires explicit intent. Requires finances:write, finances:execute and enabled review bypass. This does not answer contextual questions or approve queued action reviews.

- Domain: finances; stage: commit; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): finances:read, finances:write, finances:execute.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":false,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/finances-management.ts); typed API methods: `resolveFinanceReview`.

```json
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "action": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "approve",
        "confirm_transfer",
        "defer",
        "recategorize"
      ]
    },

      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid",
      "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$"
    },
    "confidence": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 1
    },
    "expectedTransactionUpdatedAt": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "string",
          "format": "date-time",
          "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))T(?:(?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d:[0-5]\\d(?:\\.\\d+)?(?:Z|([+-](?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d)))$"
        },
        {
          "type": "string",
          "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))T(?:(?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d(?:Z|([+-](?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d)))$"
        }
      ]

        "suggest"
      ]
    },

      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "expense",
        "income"
      ]
    },
    "rationale": {
      "default": null,

        {
          "type": "string",
```

## restore_reminder

Restore one reminder from recoverable trash using the updatedAt revision returned by delete_reminder. A concurrent change fails safely, and restoration is recorded in audit history. - Domain: reminders; stage: commit; policy: approve_each.
- Grants (all): reminders:write.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":false,"openWorldHint":false}`.

## review_mail_rule

Re-preview one saved Mail rule against the current bounded recent window. ## revoke_automation_host_schedule

Revoke this schedule's local execution binding and stored fire credentials. This does not revoke the agent token. Requires finances:read and finances:maintain. Agent mutations also require finances:write, finances:execute and enabled review bypass.

- Grants (all): finances:read, finances:maintain, finances:write, finances:execute.
