## save_domain_profile

Create or revise one domain preference profile after a short user interview. Save a draft first, preserve user wording, and use expectedVersion when updating. A saved draft is not approval. Activating Finance guidance requires finances:write, finances:execute and enabled review bypass; domain-specific API validation of source ownership, connectivity, activation authority, and preferences is authoritative.

- Domain: assistant; stage: commit; policy: approve_each.
- Grants (any): reminders:write, tasks:write, calendar:write, mail:write, goals:write, finances:write.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":false,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/assistant.ts); typed API methods: `upsertDomainProfile`.

## save_finance_onboarding_answers

Save reviewed Finance setup dispositions using exact question IDs and the current onboarding revision. Save canonical financial values through their owned operations first; answered requires actual stored evidence, none requires allowsNone, and unknown never means zero. Requires current owner-authorized Finance execution; this does not approve a budget or complete setup.

- Domain: finances; stage: commit; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): finances:write, finances:execute.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":true,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/finances.ts); typed API methods: `saveFinanceOnboardingAnswers`.

```json
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "expectedRevision": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 9007199254740990
    },
    "idempotencyKey": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 200
    },
    "answers": {
      "maxItems": 100,
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "object",
        "properties": {
          "questionId": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 160
          },
          "disposition": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "answered",
              "none",
              "unknown"
            ]
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "questionId",
          "disposition"
        ],
        "additionalProperties": false
      }
    },
    "displayPreferencesReviewed": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "automationChoice": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "later",
        "now"
      ]
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "expectedRevision",
    "idempotencyKey",
    "answers",
    "displayPreferencesReviewed",
    "automationChoice"
  ],
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

## search_calendar

Search owned calendar content and reviews. Follow returned pagination metadata; retain the same filters.

- Domain: calendar; stage: inspect; policy: read_only.
- Grants (all): calendar:read.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":true,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":true,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/workspace-settings.ts); typed API methods: `searchWorkspace`.

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "q": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 500
    },
    "offset": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 9007199254740991
    },
    "limit": {
      "default": 25,
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 100
    },
    "includeArchived": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "kind": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "all",
        "content",
        "reviews"
      ]
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "q"
  ],
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "additionalProperties": false
}
```

## send_mail_draft

Send the exact saved draft revision confirmed by confirmedUpdatedAt. Inspect recipients and content first. An uncertain delivery requires Sent Mail reconciliation before retry; sending cannot be undone. Requires mail:write. Agents also require mail:execute and enabled review bypass.

- Domain: mail; stage: commit; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): mail:write, mail:execute.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":true,"idempotentHint":false,"openWorldHint":true}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/mail-management.ts); typed API methods: `sendMailDraft`.

```json
  "properties": {

        {
          "type": "string",
          "format": "date-time",

        },
        {
          "type": "string",
          "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))T(?:(?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d(?:Z|([+-](?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d)))$"
        }
      ]
    },
    "draftId": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid",

    }
  },
```

## send_text_message

Send an SMS only after reading and understanding the conversation. Texting should convey the answer quickly: use one concise, information-dense bubble by default. Use 2–3 messages only for structured lists/data or large content the user explicitly requested. Three SMS segments require necessity; 4–6 require explicit length review; 7–10 are exceptional; over 10 is rejected. Avoid greetings, throat-clearing, repetition, markdown tables, and splitting one idea across bubbles. A provider timeout can leave a queued message with uncertain delivery; reread the conversation and do not resend it until the user or delivery status resolves the uncertainty.

- Domain: texting; stage: commit; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): texting:write.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":false,"openWorldHint":true}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/texting.ts); typed API methods: `sendTextMessage`.

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "body": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1600,
      "description": "Final concise text, without a nohmi prefix."
    },
    "contentKind": {
      "default": "concise",
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "concise",
        "essential_context",
        "structured_data",
        "requested_large_content",
        "safety_critical"
      ]
    },
    "conversationReceipt": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "description": "Receipt from the immediately preceding read_text_conversation call."
    },
    "exceptionalLengthToken": {
      "description": "Retry token returned only after the exceptional 7–10 segment stop.",
      "type": "string"
    },
    "lengthReviewToken": {
      "description": "Retry token returned after the 4–6 segment review stop.",
      "type": "string"
    },
    "necessity": {
      "description": "Why three or more segments are truly necessary.",
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 240
    },
    "seriesId": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid",
      "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$"
    },
    "seriesPart": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "maximum": 3
    },
    "seriesTotal": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 2,
      "maximum": 3
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "body",
    "conversationReceipt"
  ],
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema"
}
```

## setup_finances

Use this when the user asks to set up their finances, create or finish a financial profile, or make their first budget. Read get_finance_playbook first, then inspect existing state, return one question at a time, persist each answer, preserve unknowns or record an explicit skip, and show the first plan with its missing evidence. Budget activation binds the current profile and proposal. Agents can activate with finances:write, finances:execute and enabled review bypass using agent_self_approval; otherwise the owner approves in nohmi. Do not ask the user to name a tool.

- Domain: finances; stage: commit; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): finances:write.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":true,"openWorldHint":false}`.

## split_finance_transaction

Split one posted transaction across multiple budget categories while preserving the original economic-event lineage. Part amounts must exactly equal the original amount.

- Domain: finances; stage: commit; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): finances:write.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":true,"openWorldHint":false}`.

```json
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 200,
      "description": "Reuse only to retry the exact same mutation."

      "maxItems": 100,
```

## start_finance_account_connection

Start a secure external bank connection when the user asks to connect an account. Return the hosted HTTPS provider authorization URL directly in chat, then call complete_finance_account_connection after consent; never send the user to the nohmi web application. The external provider may still require the user to authenticate and consent. Requires finances:write, finances:execute and enabled review bypass for agents.

- Domain: finances; stage: commit; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): finances:write, finances:execute.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":true,"openWorldHint":true}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/finances.ts); typed API methods: `startFinanceAccountConnection`.

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "idempotencyKey": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 200,
      "description": "Reuse only to retry the exact same mutation."
    },
    "provider": {
      "default": "plaid",
      "type": "string",
      "const": "plaid"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "idempotencyKey"
  ],
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema"
}
```

## submit_finance_ledger_challenge

Submit complete structured coverage of a Finance maintenance candidate. Keep supported items, remove or replace corrections, and surface genuine questions or blockers. nohmi then resumes the same durable maintenance run and applies or queues the batch according to the app review setting. Required action approval is separate from challenge coverage; verify the run and its immutable period review afterward.

- Domain: finances; stage: commit; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): finances:maintain.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":true,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/finances-stewardship.ts); typed API methods: `submitFinanceLedgerChallenge`.

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "conversationEvidence": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "sourceMessageIds": {
          "minItems": 1,
          "maxItems": 100,
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "string",
            "format": "uuid",
            "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$"
          }
        },
        "explanation": {
          "type": "string",
          "minLength": 1,
          "maxLength": 4000
        }
      },

        "sourceMessageIds",
        "explanation"

      "additionalProperties": false

    },
    "challengeId": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid",
      "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$"
    },
    "checked": {
      "minItems": 12,
      "maxItems": 12,
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "enum": [
          "mixed_merchants",
          "conflicting_evidence",
          "rule_breadth",
          "prior_corrections",
          "unusual_amounts",
          "reimbursements",
          "refunds_and_transfers",
          "duplicates_and_reversals",

      "maxItems": 100,

      "items": {
        "type": "object",

          "candidateItemId": {
            "anyOf": [
              {
                "type": "string",
                "format": "uuid",
                "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$"
              },
              {
                "type": "null"
              }
            ]
          },
          "evidence": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 1000
          },
          "kind": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "correction",
              "question",
              "blocker",
              "observation"
            ]
          },
          "rationale": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 1000
          },
          "resolution": {
            "oneOf": [
              {
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {
                  "type": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "const": "keep"
                  }
                },
                "required": [
                  "type"
                ],
                "additionalProperties": false
              },
              {
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {
                  "type": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "const": "remove"
                  }
                },
                "required": [
                  "type"
                ],
                "additionalProperties": false
              },
              {
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {
                  "actionKind": {
                    "type": "string",

                      "categorization",

                    "type": "object",

                      "type": "string"
                    },

                    "type": "string",

                  }

                "accountId": {
                  "anyOf": [
                    {
                      "type": "string",
                      "format": "uuid",
                      "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$"
```

## update_attention_item

Resolve, dismiss, or reopen one nohmi attention item using the version returned by list_attention_items. If another workflow refreshed it first, nohmi returns a conflict with the current version.

- Domain: assistant; stage: commit; policy: approve_each.
- Grants (any): reminders:write, tasks:write, calendar:write, mail:write, goals:write, finances:write.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":false,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/assistant.ts); typed API methods: `updateAttentionItem`.

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "domain": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "mail",
        "calendar",
        "reminders",
        "tasks",
        "finances",
        "goals"
      ]
    },
    "id": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid",
      "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$",
      "description": "nohmi object identifier"
    },
    "expectedVersion": {
      "type": "integer",
      "exclusiveMinimum": 0,
      "maximum": 9007199254740991
    },
    "status": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "open",
        "resolved",
        "dismissed"
      ]
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "domain",
    "id",
    "expectedVersion",
    "status"
  ],
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema"
}
```

## update_calendar

Update a local calendar's name, color, or IANA timezone. Omitted fields are unchanged; null clears color. Provider calendars retain their provider's authority. Requires calendar:write.

- Domain: calendar; stage: commit; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): calendar:write.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":false,"openWorldHint":false}`.

```json
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "name": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 120
    },
    "color": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "string",
          "pattern": "^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$"
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ]
    },
    "timezone": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 100
    },
    "calendarId": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid",
      "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "calendarId"
  ],
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema"
}
```

## update_event

Update an exact event using its current revisions under the owner's granted authority. Connected writes can update an external provider and may notify existing attendees under provider semantics. Never silently move or resize a hard/non-flexible commitment.

- Domain: calendar; stage: commit; policy: approve_each.
- Grants (all): calendar:write.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":true,"idempotentHint":false,"openWorldHint":true}`.

Correct an account's name, kind, institution, manual balance, ownership, or planning inclusion. Pass its observed updatedAt as expectedUpdatedAt to reject stale edits. This never changes provider credentials or ledger history.

```json
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "accountId": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid",

      "type": [
        "number",
        "null"

        {
          "type": "string",
          "format": "date-time",
          "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))T(?:(?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d:[0-5]\\d(?:\\.\\d+)?(?:Z))$"
        },
        {
          "type": "string",
          "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))T(?:(?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d(?:Z))$"
```

## update_finance_settings

## update_finance_transaction

Correct a transaction category or note with provenance. Requires finances:write, finances:execute and enabled bypass for agents. Every agent category change, including clearing with null, requires expectedTransactionUpdatedAt from get_finance_transaction. Non-null category decisions also require confidence and rationale. learnMerchant is an explicit reusable-rule decision. Use classify_finance_transactions for reasoned batch categorization.

- Domain: finances; stage: commit; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): finances:write, finances:execute.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":true,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/finances.ts); typed API methods: `updateFinanceTransactionCanonical`.

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "category": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "string",
          "minLength": 1,
          "maxLength": 80
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ]
    },
    "confidence": {
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 1
    },
    "expectedTransactionUpdatedAt": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "string",
          "format": "date-time",
          "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))T(?:(?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d:[0-5]\\d(?:\\.\\d+)?(?:Z|([+-](?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d)))$"
        },
        {
          "type": "string",
          "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))T(?:(?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d(?:Z|([+-](?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d)))$"
        }
      ]
    },
    "learnMerchant": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "notes": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "string",
          "maxLength": 4000
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ]
    },
    "rationale": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 1000
    },
    "suggestionBasis": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "merchant_rule",
        "transaction_evidence"
      ]
    },
    "idempotencyKey": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 200,
      "description": "Reuse only to retry the exact same mutation."
    },
    "transactionId": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid",
      "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$",
      "description": "nohmi object identifier"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "idempotencyKey",
    "transactionId"
  ],
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema"
}
```

## update_mail

Update a mail conversation's read/starred state or mailbox membership using the updatedAt revision returned by list_mail or read_mail. mailboxIds is the complete desired selection from list_mailboxes for this account; an empty array removes known mailbox memberships. A stale revision is rejected; re-read before deciding whether to retry. Requires mail:write.

- Domain: mail; stage: commit; policy: approve_each.
- Grants (all): mail:write.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":false,"openWorldHint":true}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/mail.ts); typed API methods: `updateMailThread`.

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "expectedUpdatedAt": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "string",
          "format": "date-time",
          "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))T(?:(?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d:[0-5]\\d(?:\\.\\d+)?(?:Z|([+-](?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d)))$"
        },
        {
          "type": "string",
          "pattern": "^(?:(?:\\d\\d[2468][048]|\\d\\d[13579][26]|\\d\\d0[48]|[02468][048]00|[13579][26]00)-02-29|\\d{4}-(?:(?:0[13578]|1[02])-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|3[01])|(?:0[469]|11)-(?:0[1-9]|[12]\\d|30)|(?:02)-(?:0[1-9]|1\\d|2[0-8])))T(?:(?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d(?:Z|([+-](?:[01]\\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\\d)))$"
        }
      ],
      "description": "Exact updatedAt revision from the conversation that was reviewed"
    },
    "mailboxIds": {
      "maxItems": 100,
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string",
        "format": "uuid",
        "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$"
      }
    },
    "starred": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "unread": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },

      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid",
      "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$",
      "description": "nohmi object identifier"
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "expectedUpdatedAt",
    "id"
  ],
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema"
}
```

```json
    "accountId": {
```

## update_mail_settings

Patch only supplied mail preferences using the observed expectedRevision. Omitted fields are retained; null and empty selections retain their documented meanings. Agents require mail:write, mail:execute and enabled review bypass.

- Domain: mail; stage: commit; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): mail:write, mail:execute.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":false,"openWorldHint":false}`.

## update_task_list

Update one loaded List's organization fields. Pass its current expectedRevision so concurrent organization changes remain visible as API conflicts.

- Domain: tasks; stage: commit; policy: approve_each.
- Grants (all): tasks:write.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":false,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/planning.ts); typed API methods: `updateTaskList`.

```json
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "description": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "string",
          "maxLength": 10000
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ]
    },
    "color": {
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "string",
          "minLength": 1,
          "maxLength": 32

          "type": "null"

      "type": "string",

        "list",

        "star",
        "target",

        "wallet",

    "name": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 240
    },
    "expectedRevision": {
      "type": "integer",
      "exclusiveMinimum": 0,
      "maximum": 9007199254740991,
      "description": "Revision returned by the latest source-by-ID read or move preview"
    },
    "id": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid",

    "id"
  ],
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",

}
```

## update_task_settings

Patch only supplied tasks preferences using the observed expectedRevision. Omitted fields are retained; null and empty selections retain their documented meanings. Agents require tasks:write, tasks:execute and enabled review bypass.

- Domain: tasks; stage: commit; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): tasks:write, tasks:execute.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":false,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/workspace-settings.ts); typed API methods: `updateWorkspaceSettings`.

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "expectedRevision": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 0,
      "maximum": 9007199254740991
    },
    "preferences": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "includeArchivedInSearch": {
          "type": "boolean"
        },
        "taskSort": {
          "type": "string",
          "enum": [
            "default",
            "date",
            "reserved",
            "priority",
            "newest",
            "oldest",
            "title",
            "estimate"
          ]
        },
        "taskGroup": {
          "type": "string",
          "enum": [
            "none",
            "date",
            "list",
            "project"
          ]
        },
        "taskRowDetails": {
          "maxItems": 3,
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "estimate",
              "tags",
              "notes"
            ]
          }
        },
        "taskContainerSort": {
          "type": "string",
          "enum": [
            "updated",
            "name",
            "newest",
            "target"
          ]
        },
        "pinnedListIds": {
          "maxItems": 100,
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "string",
            "format": "uuid",
            "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$"
          }
        },
        "pinnedProjectIds": {
          "maxItems": 100,
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "string",
            "format": "uuid",
            "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$"
          }
        },
        "defaultCaptureListId": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "type": "string",
              "format": "uuid",
              "pattern": "^([0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[89abAB][0-9a-fA-F]{3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}|00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000|ffffffff-ffff-ffff-ffff-ffffffffffff)$"
            },
            {
              "type": "null"
            }
          ]
        },
        "showCompletedTasks": {
          "type": "boolean"
        }
      },
      "additionalProperties": false
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "expectedRevision",
    "preferences"
  ],
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "additionalProperties": false
```

## withdraw_finance_budget_revision_proposal

Withdraw the exact observed proposal revision. Requires finances:write. Agents also require finances:execute and enabled review bypass.

- Domain: finances; stage: commit; policy: approved_rule.
- Grants (all): finances:write, finances:execute.
- Compatibility: normal discovery; annotations: `{"readOnlyHint":false,"destructiveHint":false,"idempotentHint":false,"openWorldHint":false}`.
- [Adapter source](../../apps/mcp/src/tools/finances-policy-management.ts); typed API methods: `withdrawFinanceBudgetRevisionProposal`.

```json
      "maximum": 2147483647

      "type": "string",
      "format": "uuid",
```
